${displayDesc}

${t.visit}
`}).join(''); } } // --- THE BRAIN (SMART ASSISTANT) --- function processOfflineQuery(query) { const t = translations[currentLang] || translations['tr']; // 1. Normalize Query const clean = query.toLowerCase() .replace(/['".,?!]/g, '') .replace(/\s+/g, ' '); // 2. Intent Detection const isLookingForFree = clean.includes("Ã¼cretsiz") || clean.includes("free") || clean.includes("parasÄ±z"); // Category Mapping const categoryMap = { "video": ["Video"], "gÃ¶rsel": ["GÃ¶rsel", "Image", "Resim", "Ã‡izim"], "resim": ["GÃ¶rsel", "Resim"], "metin": ["Metin", "YazÄ±", "Text"], "yazÄ±": ["Metin"], "kod": ["Kodlama", "Code", "YazÄ±lÄ±m"], "ses": ["Ses", "Audio", "MÃ¼zik"], "sunum": ["Verimlilik", "Sunum"], "sohbet": ["Sohbet", "Chat"] }; let targetCategories = []; for (let key in categoryMap) { if (categoryMap[key].some(k => clean.includes(k.toLowerCase()))) { // Match found, maps loosely to internal categories // Simple heuristic: key is close enough to category name const mappedCat = Object.keys(categoryIcons).find(c => c.toLowerCase().includes(key) || key.includes(c.toLowerCase())); if (mappedCat) targetCategories.push(mappedCat); } } if (targetCategories.length === 0) targetCategories = ["Genel", "Verimlilik"]; // Default fallback // 3. Score Tools let scoredTools = tools.map(tool => { let score = 0; // Text Match if (tool.name.toLowerCase().includes(clean)) score += 20; if (JSON.stringify(tool.description).toLowerCase().includes(clean)) score += 5; // Category Match if (targetCategories.length > 0) { if (tool.categories.some(c => targetCategories.includes(c))) score += 15; } // Pricing Bonus if (isLookingForFree && (tool.pricing === 'Ãœcretsiz' || tool.pricing === 'Freemium')) { score += 10; } // Popularity/Quality Bias (Mock: ID < 20 are "popular") if (tool.id < 20) score += 2; return { tool, score }; }); // 4. Sort and Top 3 scoredTools.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score); const topResults = scoredTools.filter(item => item.score > 2).slice(0, 3); // 5. Generate Response if (topResults.length === 0) { return t.aiAssistant.noResults; } let response = `${t.aiAssistant.resultsHeader}`; topResults.forEach(item => { response += `
ğŸ”¥ ${item.tool.name} (${item.tool.pricing}): ${t.aiAssistant.viewBtn}`; }); return response; }
